All three, and the pressables, take accent="neut… ([christophehurpeau/alouette](https://github.com/christophehurpeau/alouette/tree/HEAD/packages/alouette/skills/alouette-theming) · ★1)

No variant props. Load when rendering any text; avoids font-bold and raw color classes. - **alpha-diversity-group-comparison**: Use when an amplicon feature table (BIOM or QIIME 2 format) plus a sample metadata mapping file must be tested for significant group differences in alpha-diversity indices, with boxplot figures as output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/alpha-diversity-index-calculation))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/alpha-diversity-index-calculation))
- **alpha-diversity-index-computation**: Use when a normalized (rarefied) OTU/feature table with uniform per-sample depth (e.g. 10,000 reads) is available and alpha diversity indices plus rarefaction curves are needed for downstream diversity analysis and visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/alpha-diversity-index-computation--862b62da6cb82b3b))
- **alpha-diversity-index-computation**: Use when comparing within-sample microbial diversity between treatment groups on a rarefied feature table and you need per-sample alpha-diversity estimates (including phylogenetic indices) plus covariate-adjusted group difference tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/alpha-diversity-index-computation))
- **alpha-diversity-index-interpretation**: Use when metabarcoded community samples (e.g. 16S V3-V4 / ITS2 amplicons) must be summarized by a per-sample alpha-diversity metric such as Simpson, and the values must be interpreted against water source, location, and beta-diversity patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/alpha-diversity-index-interpretation))
